Mac: always use -postEvent: in ui_controls

This CL replaces -[NSApplication sendEvent:] with -postEvent: in
ui_controls.

This simplifies the code and make the testing closer to production. For
example, NSApp.currentEvent points to a -postEvent:'ed event while it is
being dispatching. This is not true for -sendEvent:.

Historially, -sendEvent: was used only because of a quirk in AppKit that
prevents Esc key from being handled in Cocoa FindBar. Now that the Cocoa
FindBar is replaced with Views FindBar, this is no longer an issue
(details at https://crbug.com/41177633#comment15).

Also replaced a callsite of SendKeyPress with SendKeyPressToWindowSync.
The callsite was relying on -sendEvent: **synchronously** processing the
event, whereas -postEvent: processes event asynchronously.

Bug: 41177633

[glic] Resolve the window entry point CUI from the invocation

Glic.CUI.WindowEntryPointInvocation (CUI 1) was resolved from
GlicInstanceMetrics events: SUCCESS on the client acknowledging
PanelWillOpen, ABANDONED on any CloseInternal, FAILED on a set of WebUI
error states, UNKNOWN_CANCEL when the tracker was destroyed unresolved.
The invocation that the toggle starts decides the same question with a
different clock and taxonomy (ClientLoadState::kReady, GlicInvokeError),
so the two histograms disagreed in several ways:

 - SUCCESS fired at different moments (and latency differed by the
   PanelWillOpen round trip).
 - UMA kept waiting past the 60s invocation timeout and settled on
   whatever instance event happened next, possibly hours later.
 - Detach/attach and floaty replacement counted as ABANDONED, since
   they go through CloseInternal.
 - kUnresponsive was FAILED for the CUI but a usable client for the
   invocation; kSignIn never resolved the CUI at all.
 - A toggle rejected by InvokeInternal dropped the tracker and recorded
   UNKNOWN_CANCEL instead of the rejection.

Hand the tracker to the invocation's GlicInvokeMetrics instead and
resolve it there: kSuccess on RecordSuccess() (the tracker still turns a
slow success into kFailedLatency) and MapInvokeErrorToCuiOutcome() of the
error on RecordError(). CUI 1 is now equal to the structured
InvokeTerminated Outcome by construction, including for rejected
toggles and for the profile-not-enabled toggle. GlicWindowInvocationTracker
no longer interprets instance events; GlicSubmitQueryCuiTracker keeps
using that path.

ABANDONED relies on kPanelClosed (added in the previous CL) so that an
explicit close during load is still reported as such.

[SmartRestart] Respect RelaunchWindow policy for lock screen restarts

Previously, lock screen restarts (SmartRestartLockScreen) were blocked
across all managed enterprise browsers via ManagementService::IsManaged().
Per enterprise launch review, lock screen restarts should be allowed on
managed browsers by default while respecting the RelaunchWindow
enterprise policy when configured by an administrator.

[Android] Move task to front in ChromeActivityTestRule.waitForFirstFrame

On Android 17 Desktop Mode with debuggable_apks = false,
ChromeActivityTestRule.prepareUrlIntent launches ChromeLauncherActivity
(a trampoline activity) in a new task, which starts an asynchronous
DesktopTasksTransitionHandler OPEN transition in SystemUI. Because
ChromeLauncherActivity.onCreate() finishes in ~50ms without ART
debuggability overhead, it starts ChromeTabbedActivity in a second task
and finishes its own task before DesktopTasksTransitionHandler applies
its WindowContainerTransaction. When the trampoline task's transition
aborts, Android returns focus to QuickstepLauncher instead of
ChromeTabbedActivity's task, leaving ChromeTabbedActivity in
ActivityState.STOPPED with its WebContents Visibility.HIDDEN.

BaseActivityTestRule.launchActivity only waits for Stage.CREATED, and
waitForActivityCompletelyLoaded() finishes loading about:blank and
DEFERRED_STARTUP while STOPPED, causing waitForFirstFrame() to time out
because a hidden WebContents never produces a compositor frame.

In waitForFirstFrame(), if the activity is not ActivityState.RESUMED,
call ApiCompatibilityUtils.moveTaskToFront() (matching ActivityElement)
so the activity resumes and renders its first frame, and increase the
polling timeout from 3000ms to 10000ms to match surrounding startup
waits.

Bug: 572023467

mutter: Track upstream 48.4 instead of the chromium_48.4 fork

Move the Chromium-specific zcr_ui_controls_v2 implementation out of the
mutter fork branch into //third_party/mutter/chromium so that
third_party/mutter/src can point at a pristine upstream release. This
removes the need to port a set of downstream patches on every mutter
roll.

The fork's changes to upstream files are replaced as follows:
- Initialization: instead of patching meta_wayland_compositor_new(),
  the mutter executable is linked with
  -Wl,--wrap=meta_wayland_compositor_new, and the wrapper sets up
  ui_controls after the real function returns. Teardown happens via a
  weak ref on the compositor.
- MetaWaylandInput::event-handled: a clutter event filter is registered
  before mutter's own filter (MetaDisplay doesn't exist yet at that
  point). Mutter handles events synchronously from its filter, so
  requests are matched from a high-priority idle. Events that arrive
  while the stage has a grab (e.g. Wayland DnD) are queued by clutter
  and handled at the start of the next stage update, so those are
  matched after ClutterStage::after-update.
- MetaWindowDrag::update-move-done/update-resize-done: a window drag
  applies the move/resize from a META_LATER_BEFORE_REDRAW later during
  the stage update that processes the motion event, so completion is
  signaled from ClutterStage::after-update.
- clutter_virtual_input_device_release_pressed(): the virtual devices
  are recreated instead, as destroying a virtual input device already
  releases anything still pressed on it.
- The exported meta_window_actor_transform_relative_position() was
  unused and is dropped.
- The commented-out update_output_luminance_settings() is no longer
  needed since the mutter gschema is now compiled from source.
- color-management-v1.xml now comes from
  //third_party/wayland-protocols:color_management_protocol.

Tested: 514 interactive_ui_tests (TabDragging*, BookmarkBarViewTest*,
*Menu*, DesktopWidgetTestInteractive*,
DesktopWindowTreeHostPlatformImplTest*, WidgetTestInteractive*,
BrowserFocusTest*, KeyboardLockInteractiveBrowserTest*) with
--use-mutter and the mutter filter file. All pass except
MenuViewDragAndDropTest{InMenuDrag,InMenuDragNoDrop}, which fail
identically with the chromium_48.4 fork.

Parse verified_instrument_ids in GetUnmaskDetailsRequest

Adds verified_instrument_ids to payments::UnmaskDetails and parses the
repeated string-encoded int64 field from the GetDetailsForGetRealPan
response in GetUnmaskDetailsRequest. This list identifies which server
cards have already been verified on the current device for
card-on-device verification enforcement.

[omnibox][composebox] Move `aim_app.css` into `omnibox_composebox.css`

`aim_app.css` styles `cr-omnibox-composebox` from the outside
(`#composebox`, `::part()` rules) and paints the AIM popup surface and
shadow on `omnibox-aim-app`'s `#content` wrapper. Move the rules into
`omnibox_composebox.css` as `:host` / inner-element rules so the
composebox looks the same wherever it is hosted. Follow-up to
crrev.com/c/8538052.

Key changes:
- `cr-omnibox-composebox` paints the popup surface and shadow on its
  own `:host` behind a new `webuiShadowEnabled` property, which the
  host sets (`omnibox-aim-app` passes `omniboxAimWebUIShadowEnabled`).
- `aim_app.css` is deleted. Where the shared `composebox.css` styles
  the same state, the selector is repeated so the rule still wins on
  specificity and order now that it no longer comes from the outer
  scope.
- `omnibox-aim-app` loses the `#content` wrapper and the reflected
  properties that only existed for `aim_app.css`. It still reflects
  `webui-shadow-enabled_` for `omnibox_popup_document_style.css`.
- `aim_app_test.ts` covers the host-passed `webuiShadowEnabled`;
  comments in `omnibox_popup_shared_style.css` are updated to match.

No intended behavior change.

Demo:
http://screencast/cast/NTAzMDY2NTcxNTE4NzcxMnw5NGRiMzM2NC0yMw

[ash] Cancel window cycling before Shell teardown

If an Alt+Tab window cycling session remains active into ChromeOS
shutdown, `WindowCycleList::~WindowCycleList()` is destroyed late in
`Shell::~Shell()` when `window_cycle_controller_` is reset. By that
point in teardown:
1. `ChromeBrowserMainExtraPartsAsh::PostMainMessageLoopRun()` has
   already destroyed the `ash::TabScrubber` singleton, so calling
   `ChromeShellDelegate::SetTabScrubberEnabled(true)` dereferences a
   null `TabScrubber::GetInstance()` pointer.
2. `Shell::~Shell()` has already reset `event_rewriter_controller_`, and
   `window_cycle_controller_` is mid-destruction.

An active `WindowCycleList` can survive into `Shell::~Shell()` when all
cycled windows are destroyed during the initial 150ms UI delay before
`cycle_view_` is created (because `OnWindowDestroying()` only checked
`windows_.empty()` inside `if (cycle_view_)`), or when cycling in
per-active-desk mode on an empty desk while windows exist on inactive
desks.

Fix this across all three layers:
- Cancel any active window cycling in
  `WindowCycleList::OnWindowDestroying()` whenever `windows_` becomes
  empty, even if `cycle_view_` has not been created yet.
- Guard `WindowCycleController::CancelCycling()` with `IsCycling()` and
  call it early in `Shell::~Shell()` before `CloseAllAppWindows()` and
  `event_rewriter_controller_.reset()`.
- Null-check `TabScrubber::GetInstance()` in
  `ChromeShellDelegate::SetTabScrubberEnabled()`.

Bug: b:572783887
Test: ash_unittests --gtest_filter=*WindowCycleControllerTest.DestroyAllWindowsDuringInitialDelayCancelsCycling*:*WindowCycleControllerShutdownTest.ShellDestructionCancelsActiveWindowCycling*
Test: unit_tests --gtest_filter=*ChromeShellDelegateUnitTest.SetTabScrubberEnabledHandlesNullTabScrubber*

🌺 Revert "[Android] Fix flaky failures in VoiceToolbarButtonControllerTest"

This reverts commit 3b1ab97e6001d27eb1a5ade609781e3e1f04caed.
Culprit: 3b1ab97e6001 - "[Android] Fix flaky failures in VoiceToolbarButtonControllerTest" (refs/heads/main@{#1718101}, https://crrev.com/c/8563359)

Reason for revert: consistently fails android-internal-desktop-x64-rel.

Mechanism: In testVoiceButtonInToolbarIsDisabledOnNtp, replacing
mActivityTestRule.loadUrl(getOriginalNativeNtpUrl()) with
mPage.loadPageProgrammatically(getOriginalNativeNtpUrl(),
RegularNewTabPageStation.newBuilder()) fails deterministically on
Desktop Android (both android-internal-desktop-x64-rel and
android-desktop-17-x64-rel-emu-tests build 4892). On Desktop Android,
UrlOverrideUtils.isWebUiNtpOverrideEnabled() is true, so
RegularNewTabPageStation expects the tab URL to contain
UrlConstantResolver.getOriginalNtpUrl() ("chrome://newtab/"), whereas
getOriginalNativeNtpUrl() loads "chrome-native://newtab/".

VoiceToolbarButtonControllerTest#testVoiceButtonInToolbarIsDisabledOnNtp
has failed in 3/3 recent builds of android-internal-desktop-x64-rel,
every attempt in every one of them. This is not flake: it reproduces on
every run, so it blocks the CQ for every unrelated CL that touches this
suite. The regression appears at refs/heads/main@{#1718101}.

Example failing run:
https://ci.chromium.org/ui/p/chrome/builders/try/android-internal-desktop-x64-rel/31211

To run/deflake this test on CQ before relanding, add the following
footer to your CL description:
  Include-Ci-Only-Tests: chrome.android.desktop:android-desktop-17-x64-rel-emu-tests,chromium.android.desktop:android-desktop-x64-rel-15-tests|chrome_public_test_apk

Reverting to green the tree first. Please re-land once the failure is
understood and covered; this revert is about restoring CQ throughput,
not about the merit of the change.

[Reading Mode] Cancel a pending IRM print when IRM closes

When a tab closes or navigates while a print is pending, Chrome cancels
the print. Closing IRM didn't cancel the print, because the IRM
WebContents stays alive after IRM closes, so the print could still go
out after IRM closed.

Cancel the IRM print preview when IRM closes, as Chrome does for a tab.
To do this, add PrintPreviewDialogController::CancelPrintPreview() and
call it from ~ScopedTabPrintTargetOverride().

> [js_sandbox] Enable BackupRefPtr in the sandbox process
>
> The js_sandbox service process does not go through ContentMain, so it
> never enabled the memory protections provided by partition alloc. This
> CL enables BackupRefPtr for the js-sandbox process.
>
> Additionally, since partition alloc has a bunch of dependencies, we make
> the following changes in the sandbox process:
>
> - Initialize feature flags.
> - Add a value for the command line process type switch ("js-sandbox").
> - Associate the main looper with a task runner.
>
> `kPartitionAllocMemoryTagging` is kept disabled to match WebView
> behaviour.
>

Enable Redirection Guard for Windows services

tree
3de61e3ea86b71c3ad4389ae3555055c88ed94ae
parent
61f8737443ab7c486217bcb3d5ce5fa36038b21d [ diff ] 
Enable Redirection Guard for Windows services

The elevation service runs as SYSTEM and touches the filesystem for
medium-integrity COM callers: it unpacks and deletes the recovery
directory, and reads DPAPI master keys for App-Bound Encryption while
impersonating the caller. Redirecting one of those paths with a planted
NTFS junction would give an arbitrary write or delete as SYSTEM.

This is defense in depth. It adds a second, OS-enforced control so that
a future change introducing a user-writable path is not an elevation of
privilege on its own.

Enable Redirection Guard unconditionally from ServiceProgramMain(), so
it applies to every service built on it. Windows versions without it
fail with ERROR_INVALID_PARAMETER or ERROR_NOT_SUPPORTED, which are
expected and not logged.

Policy reference:
https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows/win32/api/winnt/ns-winnt-process-mitigation-redirection-trust-policy

Bug: 557568153
Change-Id: Id0fa1f10f33e47497ef5b223437e5ede4b1896aa

040399974b8e01287af9a7ae425e029ad7036d06
parent
35924b0becf3274fb2a265d0ac07157df616c63a [ diff ] 
[forms] Drop duplicate validity invalidation in SubtreeHasChanged

SetValueFromRenderer() calls SetNeedsValidityCheck(), which invalidates
kPseudoValid/Invalid/UserValid/UserInvalid when validity turns dirty.
SubtreeHasChanged() then invalidated the same four unconditionally.

When the flag is already dirty, an earlier SetNeedsValidityCheck() has
invalidated them and nothing has recomputed validity since, so the
pending invalidation still stands and the repeat is redundant.

Bug: none

Roll third_party/glib from 2.85.4 to 2.90.1

2.85.4 is an unstable development snapshot; 2.90.1 is the latest stable
release. All mutter versions through 51.x only require glib >= 2.81.1,
so glib can be rolled independently of mutter.

Changes:
- Bump DEPS and README.chromium to 2.90.1 (e05063ccc6c8).
- Regenerate checked-in headers and sources with on_update.sh.
- Add new gio sources to BUILD.gn: giptosmessage, gipv6tclassmessage
  and gnetworkmonitorsystemd.
- on_update.sh: also #undef HAVE_FREE_SIZED, HAVE_FREE_ALIGNED_SIZED,
  HAVE_LIBELF and G_HAVE_FREE_SIZED. free_sized() and
  free_aligned_sized() are detected on hosts with a recent glibc but are
  not available in the Debian bullseye sysroot, and libelf is not a
  dependency of the GN build.

parent
86a9b9678cfc9b791eadbb301510c5611fd7abd3 [ diff ] 
[gpu] Add ShaderDiskCacheBackendExperiment for shader cache.

Adds the kShaderDiskCacheBackendExperiment feature (disabled by default)
with a "type" parameter ("default" or "simple", defaulting to
"simple") to evaluate switching the GPU shader disk cache
(net::SHADER_CACHE) to SimpleCache across platforms.

While GpuPersistentCache, SkiaGraphite, and
SkiaGraphiteUsePersistentCache move some GPU caches to PersistentCache,
GpuDiskCache is still used on all platforms (e.g. WebGL/WebGPU caches).

To ensure a fair comparison across active experiment arms:
1. Feature disabled (No-op): uses net::CACHE_BACKEND_DEFAULT without
   wiping existing shader caches.
2. Feature enabled, type = "default" (Status quo + wipe): wipes each
   shader cache directory once on first open and recreates it with
   net::CACHE_BACKEND_DEFAULT.
3. Feature enabled, type = "simple" (SimpleCache + wipe): wipes each
   shader cache directory once on first open and recreates it with
   net::CACHE_BACKEND_SIMPLE.

The one-time wipe per shader cache directory is tracked in the cache
index header (IndexHeader::experiment in BackendImpl and
FakeIndexData::zero in SimpleBackendImpl).

[contextual_tasks] Support dark mode in lens

Replaces the prefers-color-scheme CSS media queries with a host-level
dark-mode attribute. The darkMode property reflects as an attribute and
initializes based on the presence of the "cs=1" URL query parameter.
Also adds unit tests covering the dark mode attribute reflection and URL
parameter parsing.

[inline-layout] Monospace (11/18) Add tests

Add end-to-end coverage for compact monospace storage using a real
fixed-pitch font. Compare character and caret positions with compaction
enabled and disabled, and verify that computing ink bounds leaves glyph
storage compact.

Cover nested views after letter spacing materializes glyph data. Verify
that views created before materialization can still iterate and copy the
updated glyphs correctly.

No behavioral changes are expected.

Mark CrossDeviceThemeSyncBridge as active after initial sync metadata

Previously, CrossDeviceThemeSyncBridge::OnSyncStarting immediately ran
sync_started_cb_, transitioning CrossDeviceThemeTracker to kActive even
before MergeFullSyncData received initial theme protos from the Sync
server on initial sign-in.

Wait until change_processor()->IsTrackingMetadata() is true (either on
OnSyncStarting / OnReadAllDataAndMetadata when metadata is already
persisted, or after MergeFullSyncData completes initial sync) before
invoking sync_started_cb_. Also transition back to kInitializing on
OnSyncStarting when metadata is not yet tracked, and handle
OnSyncPaused.

[AF] Create AutofillEnableOAuth2TokenFix flag

This CL introduces the AutofillEnableOAuth2TokenFix feature flag to fix
issues with stale OAuth2 tokens sent in payments requests. These issues
can be fixed by relying on `IdentityManager`'s access token cache rather
than keeping an internal cache in `PaymentsAccessTokenFetcher` and
`PaymentsNetworkInterfaceBase`.

For more info, see go/gdfsc-missing-gaia.

[Actor] Migrate observation voting to ActorSurfaceHandle.

This CL updates the ExecutionEngine and TabObservationStrategy to use
ActorSurfaceHandle instead of tabs::TabHandle for tracking observation
votes (screenshots and page content extraction).

Key changes:
- Updated TabObservationStrategy to key its internal vote maps by
  ActorSurfaceHandle.
- Modified ExecutionEngine to resolve the pre-invoke navigation ID and
  cast observation votes using the request's ActorSurface.
- Updated TabObservationController to resolve ActorSurfaceHandles from
  TabHandles during observation.
- Migrated associated unit and browser tests.

This is a mechanical migration. No behavior change is intended.

[BottomSheet] Deflake BottomSheetObserverTest#testOffsetChangedEvent
📋 Metadata
commit
2c42fd804b7d164d4de0e7eab17459e556b7c2f4
[ log ]
author
Charles Hager <clhager@google.com>
Wed Oct 14 02:15:54 2026
committer
Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Wed Oct 14 02:15:54 2026
tree
d904aabdf120a6cf9a737c4257be6e8a27183ba0
parent
c872761b32a3034591d63601cc43526371f72737 [ diff ] 
[BottomSheet] Deflake BottomSheetObserverTest#testOffsetChangedEvent

testOffsetChangedEvent moved the sheet with setSheetOffsetFromBottom()
in a UI thread task, then read the value sent to onSheetOffsetChanged()
from the instrumentation thread. setSheetOffsetFromBottom() only moves
the sheet; its state stays FULL. A layout pass that runs in between
re-settles the sheet to the height of its current state, e.g. the
toolbar size change on the sheet's first layout after setUp() calls
setSheetState(getSheetState(), false). The sheet then moves back to the
full height and sends 1, which the test read instead:

java.lang.AssertionError: expected:<0.0> but was:<1.0>

This is not a rounding error: the hidden ratio is 0, so the hidden
offset is exactly 0, and 1 is the fraction for the full height.

crrev.com/c/8031671 made the third assert recompute its expected value
from the sheet's offset after the fact. That value follows a re-settled
sheet instead of preventing the re-settle, and the browser controls
offset it accounts for is always 0 here because TestBottomSheetContent
does not hide on scroll.

Set the state, move the sheet and read each sent value in a single UI
thread task so that no layout pass can run in between, and check that
each move notifies observers synchronously. The midpoint is expected to
send exactly 0.5 again. Also drop the getOffsetFromBrowserControls()
accessor the previous fix added to BottomSheetTestSupport, and make the
coordinator method private again.

6c62597be713a908baf09d25e5770c5bffe9d377
parent
6947a8a5d3142dda5a42e0eeef24700ef7be5c69 [ diff ] 
gin: Add compile-time dispatch for bound functions and properties

SetMethod(name, &T::Method) wraps the pointer in a base::RepeatingCallback
held by a cppgc CallbackHolder, and every call unwraps the holder, checks
its signature id and runs the callback through two indirect calls.

Add SetMethod<&T::Method>(name), SetProperty<&T::get[, &T::set]>(name),
SetLazyDataProperty<&T::get>(name) and CreateFunctionTemplate<kTarget>().
The V8 callback converts the arguments with the existing ArgumentHolder
machinery and calls the target directly; there is no holder. Targets of
the same signature share the conversion code. Receivers still come from
`this`, and the "Illegal invocation ... of type X" message is kept by
storing the type name as the template's data, read only on failure.

The value-taking overloads are unchanged; call sites move over in
follow-ups. gin_unittests micro-benchmark (not included), Linux x64
release, ns per call, value form -> template form:

  void()                    42.3 -> 17.3
  int()                     43.8 -> 18.4
  void(int, int)            47.2 -> 22.0
  void(const std::string&)  72.2 -> 49.8

The wrappable tests now run each case through both forms.

Bug: None

[crubit] Support `cpp_api_from_rust` with a prebuilt Rust std library.

e65a87e428e2e1a2ea3aeab692ab3d7dce396eca

tree
b1f3c46d6ca81046756068b4c52871f5e7de3e24
parent
8e29dd0baa0535fda23d4172b87a9ca99e00d773 [ diff ] 
[crubit] Support `cpp_api_from_rust` with a prebuilt Rust std library.

Before this CL, `cpp_api_from_rust` (Crubit's `cc_bindings_from_rs`)
required the in-tree build of the Rust standard library, because the
C++ bindings of the stdlib (`rs_core.h`, `rs_alloc.h`, `rs_std.h`) were
generated only from the stdlib sources in //build/rust/std/rules.  This
meant that:

* `gn gen` failed for builds with a custom Rust toolchain (set via
  `rust_sysroot_absolute`), because //components/cbor, Blink fonts,
  //components/web_package and other code unconditionally depend on
  Crubit-generated bindings.
* `cpp_api_from_rust` could not be used in the toolchain for Rust host
  build tools.

After this CL, the C++ bindings of a prebuilt stdlib are generated
from the `.rmeta` files in the sysroot, using the rmeta interface of
`cc_bindings_from_rs` (via a new `use_rmeta_interface` parameter of
the `cpp_api_from_rust.gni` template).  `enable_cpp_api_from_rust` no
longer depends on `use_chromium_rust_toolchain` nor on
`rust_prebuilt_stdlib`.

`//build/rust/tests` now also generates and compiles (but does not link
or run) C++ bindings in the toolchain for Rust host build tools, which
always uses a prebuilt stdlib.  This provides lightweight test coverage
of the new code path on regular bots.

Tested:
* Default config: `build/rust/tests:deps` builds (including the new
  host-toolchain test), and `base_unittests
  --gtest_filter=RustCcBindingsFromRs.*` passes.
* `rust_crubit_root` pointing at a copy of Crubit from
  //third_party/rust-toolchain, and `enable_rust_api_from_cpp = true`,
  both with the Chromium toolchain and with a custom toolchain
  (`rust_sysroot_absolute` pointing at //third_party/rust-toolchain,
  `is_component_build = false`): `base_unittests
  --gtest_filter='RustCcBindingsFromRs.*:TestRustApiFromCpp.*'` passes.

blink: Fix moves that silently fall back to copies

Two small defects that each cost an avoidable copy:

- InlineBoxState's move constructor takes const InlineBoxState&&, so
  state is a const lvalue inside it and the std::move on
  pending_descendants selects Vector's copy constructor. Drop the const,
  which the comment on the declaration already assumes.

- MutableCSSPropertyValueSet wraps each entry in an explicit
  CSSPropertyValue temporary before appending it. UncheckedAppend takes
  a forwarding reference, so pass the entry directly.

No behavior change.

[Reading mode] Pass font size to the font size menu as a prop

font-size-reset event. The app writes the pref, logs the metric and
passes the size back down. The menu announces the change once the prop
updates, so clamped min/max clicks are still not announced.

497612796be6c3ce53e4b6f0a059199762a3f740 [ diff ] 
Move CrossDeviceSettingImporter init to initProfileDependentFeatures

Move CrossDeviceSettingImporter creation out of the
TabbedRootUiCoordinator constructor (preInflationStartup) and into
initProfileDependentFeatures(), which runs once both native
initialization has finished and the Profile is available, after other
profile-dependent features such as NtpSyncedThemeManager are
initialized.

CrossDeviceSettingImporter is profile-dependent and cannot import
settings before native and profile initialization complete.

bd848a6eaa3d5c9cebd34b471593afdd38cd2347

1a9f5668786e892f8b856ef7af4a2c05f8d1611f [ diff ] 
[crubit] Add `rust_crubit_root` GN arg for system-provided Crubit.

Before this CL, the GN build always took Crubit tools
(`cc_bindings_from_rs`, `rs_bindings_from_cc`) and Crubit support
libraries from //third_party/rust-toolchain.  This does not work for
builds that use a custom Rust toolchain via `rust_sysroot_absolute`,
because Crubit has to be built from the same Rust compiler sources as
`rustc`.

After this CL, a new `rust_crubit_root` GN arg (similar to
`rust_bindgen_root`) tells the build where to find Crubit.  It defaults
to //third_party/rust-toolchain when using the Chromium toolchain.  When
using a custom toolchain, it has to be set explicitly, and GN reports a
clear error message if Crubit is needed but the arg is not set.

Crubit-generated code is formatted (with `rustfmt` and `clang-format`)
only when using Crubit from //third_party/rust-toolchain.  Formatting
only makes the generated code easier to read, and the formatting tools
may not be available when using a system-provided Crubit.

There is no change in the default configuration (`gn gen` produces
identical `.ninja` files).

e1ca357fac620926b4011532e0c49565c2df2858 [ diff ] 
[Media] Disallow mixing opaque and non-opaque responses in MultiBuffer

Fail the load in ResourceMultiBufferDataProvider::DidReceiveResponse()
if a resource that has already received a response receives a subsequent
response whose CORS cross-origin taint state differs from the earlier
response. This is guarded by a new kDisallowMixedOpaqueMediaResponses
kill switch (enabled by default).

The behavior of mixing multiple range responses via a Service Worker is
not defined by the HTML or Service Worker specifications
(https://github.com/w3c/ServiceWorker/issues/703), and both Firefox and
Safari already reject mixing opaque and non-opaque range responses with
a load error. Update the existing web test and WPT expectations to match
this behavior across all browsers.

Make Skia's shared context current before pruning transfer cache.
📋 Metadata

0960c42b6cfe91903e40dc2d13c36a2742b482b5
[ log ]
author
Ken Russell <kbr@chromium.org>

tree
2737402671c95df7d083ddce5ddea308d55969b2
parent
624029e566d53c2c7334e79bfb30b628c04ab2f7 [ diff ] 
Make Skia's shared context current before pruning transfer cache.

Several entry points into the ServiceTransferCache could cause old
entries to be iterated and pruned, which - through a long series of
virtual calls going into Skia's Ganesh backend - could cause GL
objects like buffers to be deleted with the wrong context current (in
this case, a user's WebGL context).

Address this problem inside the ServiceTransferCache by providing it
the SharedContextState, which is guaranteed to outlive the transfer
cache due to object ownership, instead of passing in the Ganesh or
Graphite context while adding specific entries. Make the context
current in PruneOldEntries. Assert the context is current in other key
entry points.

Deeper context verification was considered and rejected because of the
possibility of performance regressions on critical platforms.

Add unit tests ensuring that objects are deleted with the correct
context current, and a GPU integration test which reliably reproduces
a GPU process crash without the fix.

Co-authored with Gemini.

3f01f54a9ef1cca8d8af7360403c9dcabaa1e458
parent
cdfe11f1a1c3cb95b03a5630d61a359c6ba6a38d [ diff ] 
Avoid stalling Cast Streaming silent video.

In Cast Streaming (mirroring and remoting) sessions, senders frequently
negotiate both audio and video in the OFFER/ANSWER exchange even when
streaming silent video or slideshows where zero audio packets are
transmitted. Because Starboard's SbPlayer pipeline waits for all
configured streams to preroll before advancing media time and
presenting frames, creating SbPlayer with an active audio stream when
no audio buffers arrive causes video playback to hang indefinitely on
the first frame.

Update StreamingReceiverSessionClient to pass the modified audio/video
capability constraints to InitializeReceiverSession, and update
MediaPipelineBackendStarboard in streaming mode (when both audio and
video decoders exist) to create SbPlayer as video-only until the first
non-EOS audio buffer is pushed. If an audio buffer arrives after
initialization, recreate SbPlayer with both audio and video while
re-queueing any in-flight video sample and preserving player state,
volume, in-band resolution, and cumulative frame statistics.

Bug: 534195232
Test: starboard_media_unittests, StreamingReceiverSessionClientTest, and Cast Streaming with silent and audio+video streams

f32fc44e2414fc84a8da8629d767eae6ef8fcd49
parent
6638aa0f7d68d0e46dc61290ae2f938534ebe159 [ diff ] 
settings: Keep search result open when switching back to its tab

When settings is shown in a tab and the user opens a page from the
search results in two-column mode, switching to another tab and back
dropped the page and showed the search results again.

Switching tabs detaches the settings view and re-attaches it on the way
back, and CompositorViewHolder then calls requestFocus() on the page.
That focuses the first focusable descendant, which in the search results
state is the search query field. Its focus listener treats focus as a
tap on the field and pops the back stack to the search results.

Keep the query field from taking focus until the re-attach has finished,
so only real user interaction returns to the search results.

Bug: 573289137, 521895796
Test: chrome_public_test_apk SettingsPageTest

[CodeHealth] Migrate AudioBufferTest to AudioBuffer spans

`media::AudioBuffer` is deprecating `channel_data()` and `channels()` in
favor of modern span accessors (`planar_data()`, `planar_channel()`,
`interleaved_data()`, and `bitstream_data()`). `audio_buffer_unittest.cc`
still exercised the deprecated accessors across multiple tests.

This CL updates `AudioBufferTest` to use modern span accessors,
eliminating raw pointer arithmetic and replacing `memset()` with
`std::ranges::fill()`.

TAG=agy

ddbab381e7262dda28130c4aa00fde3e24429849

75ab8be0cbae4eabbcf4a1e93df2236ca6f81738 [ diff ] 
[glic] Rename Glic.Session.*Crash histograms to Glic.Contents.*

Glic.Session.WebClientCrash.ExitReason and
Glic.Session.WebUiCrash.TerminationStatus are recorded by the glic host
whenever the web client or WebUI renderer process goes away. They are
not tied to the deprecated single-instance glic session; the "Session"
in their names is a leftover.

Rename them to Glic.Contents.WebClientCrash.ExitReason and
Glic.Contents.WebUiCrash.TerminationStatus, next to the other
Glic.Contents.* metrics recorded by the glic WebUI and web client
contents managers, and remove the old names.

The new Glic.Contents.WebClientCrash.ExitReason summary describes the
actual recording point (the web client's render process going away)
rather than the webview `exit` event.

OBSOLETE_HISTOGRAM[Glic.Session.WebClientCrash.ExitReason]=Renamed to Glic.Contents.WebClientCrash.ExitReason.
OBSOLETE_HISTOGRAM[Glic.Session.WebUiCrash.TerminationStatus]=Renamed to Glic.Contents.WebUiCrash.TerminationStatus.

76fc6588355714b9f4f99ca509d6b4e7226e924b [ diff ] 
[Glic] Record Glic.WebContentsContextMenu on Android and for images

CL 2 of adding invocation source split for glic metrics.

Glic.WebContentsContextMenu.{GlicContextType} records whether the Glic
context menu item was executed or ignored when the menu closes, but only
desktop records it, and only for the "open Glic" item. Android has no
impression metric for its Ask Gemini context menu items, and the share
image item isn't covered on either platform.

Record it on Android from ChromeContextMenuPopulator.onMenuClosed(),
once for each Glic item shown in the menu: Link or Page for the Ask
Gemini item and a new Image variant for the image item. A link that
wraps an image shows both items on Android, so it records both. The Ask
Gemini click still invokes Glic with kWebContentsContextMenu (23); a
follow-up switches it to Link or Page.

Desktop records its share image item as Image too, so every variant
except Link and the text selection ones is now recorded on both
platforms. Existing desktop Page and TextSelection samples are
unchanged; their recording just moves into a shared helper.

diagnostics: Declare touchpad Polymer property in TouchpadTesterElement

Register 'touchpad' in static get properties() and mark the class field
with 'declare' in TouchpadTesterElement.

When useDefineForClassFields is enabled, an undeclared class field
defines an own property on the instance that shadows Polymer's
prototype accessors and breaks template bindings to 'touchpad'.

[UndoTabClosures] Use tab ids in TabGridDialog undo snackbar payloads

TabGridDialogMediator's multi-tab undo snackbar held the closing
List<Tab> as its action data. Store the tab ids instead
(TabModelUtils.getTabIds, same order), dismiss with the matching id
list, and read ids in onAction/onDismissNoAction. The single-tab payload
was already a tab id.

Dismissal matching is unchanged: Tab has no equals(), so the old
List<Tab> matched by identity in order, which is equivalent to matching
the id lists built from the same lists. Undo and commit call the same
TabModel methods with the same ids. No behavior change.

NTP: Remove unused realbox hadSecondarySide

hadSecondarySide was only read by the
:host([realbox-can-show-secondary-side][realbox-had-secondary-side])
selector in app.css, which has been redundant since
crrev.com/c/6021963 simplified the other selector in the same list to
:host([realbox-can-show-secondary-side]).

This change removes it from <cr-searchbox-dropdown>, <ntp-searchbox>
and <ntp-app>. It also removes the has-secondary-side binding from
<ntp-searchbox> to the dropdown, since the dropdown computes and
reflects it itself.

No behavior change.

fe28bf944a5071097eb890fc802fc59313ba1443 [ diff ] 
[Actor] Validate tabs added to ActorTask only through their surface

AddTab() and ObserveTabOnce() checked the tab's profile before
forwarding to the surface methods, and Act()'s early add of the actions'
tabs went through AddTab(). The surface methods already reject a surface
that is gone or belongs to another profile, so drop the tab checks. A
tab in a profile without an ActorKeyedService (e.g. incognito) has no
surface, so it is now reported as kTabWentAway instead of
kActionTargetCrossProfile, the affected tests are updated.

parent

Remove misnamed/unused audio capture-stream test macros

The MAYBE_ macro block for KillProcessZerosAudioCaptureStreams was
copy-pasted from KillProcessZerosVideoCaptureStreams: the macro was
misnamed as a duplicate of the video test's macro, and the audio test
was declared with its raw name. As a result the IS_ANDROID -> DISABLED_
guard never actually applied to the audio test.

The audio test has been enabled for ~2 years with no flakiness, so the
Android disable was an oversight that never took effect rather than a
needed workaround. Delete the dead, misnamed macro block and the stale
"flaky on Android" comment, leaving the test enabled on all platforms.
This removes the duplicate macro definition with no loss of coverage.

Test-only change.

parent
8b257ed664534e2e46064e476f7d5d74ef7d5a02 [ diff ] 
Fix commitStyles crash on replaced+canceled animation

Running commitStyles on a canceled animation has no effect as the
animation has no effect values. However, a code path for committing
styles on a replaced animation assumed that the animation wasn't
canceled, as KeyframeEffect::ApplyEffects assumes a valid progress.

This CL simply skips the calculation in the case where there is no
progress, making commitStyles effectively a NOP, as it ought to be in
this case.

author
Patrick Monette <pmonette@chromium.org>

tree
55372786fd2b8107cf5111130c6508d7394808b5
parent
c896aa591728327aa27be879bf934dc1a8f59b46 [ diff ] 
[blob] Don't stop NotifyMemoryItemsUsed() at an item being paged

NotifyMemoryItemsUsed() returned early when it found an item that was
being paged to disk. The remaining items in the list were then not
added to, or refreshed in, the list of items that can be paged. They
would only be tracked again the next time the blob was used.

Skip only the item being paged and keep processing the rest.

8bd9db121c2f0614b90034bd18b80a4e12187d01
parent
14f0ea0127d782f2a474c370aec0bb982bfbd90d [ diff ] 
[UndoTabClosures] Capture undo snackbar state at close time

Adds UndoClosureSnapshot, an immutable record of a closure (closed tab
ids, titles and group ids, group titles, hiding and surviving groups,
sync state) captured when the closure starts. UndoClosureSnapshotFactory
builds snapshots from the tab model or from saved tab groups, and
UndoBarContentFactory derives an UndoBarContent (the snapshot plus the
snackbar text and UMA type) from a snapshot. UndoBarController,
TabUndoBarController and SavedTabGroupUndoBarController now use them:
the controller queue holds snapshots directly, and the UndoBarContent is
the snackbar's action data, so the undo snackbar no longer holds live
Tabs. This replaces UndoActionData and the TabClosureEvent wrapper.

Also removes TabUndoBarController's ClosureMetadata and three
dismissSnackbars(this, <tab ids>) calls that could never match: the undo
snackbar's action data was always an UndoActionData, which does not
override equals(), so SnackbarCollection's Objects.equals() check never
succeeded. The snackbar staying visible after an undo or commit from
outside the snackbar is pre-existing and unchanged here.

No intended behavior change, except that text and UMA for throttled
snackbars now reflect the state at close time rather than at show time.

tree
711989d56c25920dcdc87779a541e65a7206c583
parent
147bc1e52498b2780e7afd8d9a75d82bd5fef65c [ diff ] 
Stop neon_2_sse from suppressing deprecation warnings in dependents

neon_2_sse_flags is a public config that added
-Wno-deprecated-declarations on x64. Through tflite's public deps, it
was applied to most of //chrome (e.g. via
//chrome/browser:browser_public_dependencies ->
//components/autofill/core/browser -> //third_party/tflite), so use of
deprecated APIs (e.g. gmock's single-argument testing::Invoke()) built
cleanly on all x64 builders but failed on non-x64 builders like
linux-arm64-dbg, causing reverts.

NEON_2_SSE.h only uses the deprecated attribute to flag slow emulated
intrinsics as performance warnings, and provides
NEON2SSE_DISABLE_PERFORMANCE_WARNING to turn those off. Use that instead
of the warning flag.

Also fix the existing deprecated gmock/gtest usages in ChromeOS-only
tests that this uncovered: single-action DoAll(), single-argument
Invoke(), and INSTANTIATE_TEST_CASE_P.

899c85c24afc98cded1f99a954fd6e468afeee70

7fe5663db29b7f26f77eb7dd053acfb367d7d599 [ diff ] 
[style] Default GridTrackList's copy assignment

The hand-written operator= copied all eight members verbatim, which is
exactly what the defaulted copy constructor does. A member added later
would be handled by one and missed by the other.

It also returned void; defaulting it returns a reference, as the
convention expects.

Default it in the .cc file rather than in the class. A function defaulted
on its first declaration is implicitly inline, which would expand the
member-wise copy into every ComputedStyle track-list setter call site.

d64a76dce84eb3c23c168f77f13611064ba24dce
parent
14e372a1cb2f34cb876e6f2713a07748ff02e768 [ diff ] 
[glic] Abandon show-only invocations when their panel is closed

Closing the panel while the client is still loading did not end the
invocation that opened it. For a toolbar-button toggle that meant the
invocation ran on until it timed out (kTimeout, counted as FAILED_LATENCY)
or until blank instance removal happened to destroy the instance, while
Glic.CUI.WindowEntryPointInvocation records exactly this case as
ABANDONED. The two could therefore never agree on abandonment.

Add GlicInvokeError::kPanelClosed (-> GlicCuiOutcome::kAbandoned). When
the active embedder is explicitly closed before the client is ready
(Host::client_load_state() != kReady), GlicInstanceImpl asks the
coordinator to cancel the instance's show-only invocations with it.

Scope is deliberately narrow:
 - Only the active embedder counts. Closing an inactive side panel, or the
   old embedder while the conversation moves to another surface
   (detach/attach, floaty replacement), does not take the UI away.
 - Backgrounding (tab switch, kBackgrounded / kPeek) is not a close.
 - Invocations that will send something to the web client keep running,
   as the client may still act on them; this is the existing behaviour
   covered by ClientInvokeKeepsBlankInstanceAlive.
 - Once the client is ready a close no longer affects the invocation.

The two NeverReadyClient browser tests that asserted the old
kInstanceDestroyedBlankInstanceClosed outcome now see kPanelClosed, and
a new one checks that a tab switch does not end the invocation.

Reland "Add Android built-in summarize solution config"

This is a reland of commit 4b08c21fa36773de04668eb4e3e8eebd06367c69 but
with flaky tests fixed. This reland removes automatic fake adaptation
injection from the Android AI test fixture. Tests now use the built-in
config consistently, avoiding asynchronous adaptation loading racing
with availability checks and session creation.

Disable CreateSummarizerWithTextSafetyCheck under crbug.com/571742381
until its safety-required config can be reliably applied before session
creation. Other logic are automatically cherry-picked and unchanged.

Original change's description:
> Add Android built-in summarize solution config
>
> Provide a built-in solution config for Android on-device summarize web
> API by reusing the shared Summarizer config introduced by CL 8419381 and
> applying Android MLKit/AICore specific token limits and text-safety
> settings.
>
> Introduce AndroidManifest to load builtin solution config for web API
> like Summarize but retain the OptimizationGuideModelProvider based
> adaptation delivery for existing Chrome features like Scam Detection.
>
> A follow-up CL in the future may add component-updater config delivery
> and allow compatible component configs to override the built-in
> fallback.
>
> Binary size increases primarily because Android now links the shared
> Summarizer solution-config builder and its production prompt templates.

> built-in fallback.

[Extensions] Migrate some tests from browserAction -> action

The browserAction API was replaced with the action API in MV3, but they
share a lot of the same functionality. Much of this functionality has
dedicated action-api-based tests, but there's some that's only actively
exercised with the browserAction API (because the underlying code is
effectively shared).

Migrate some of these tests to be based on the action API instead of the
browserAction API.

Note: Some of these were updated to both use best practices (e.g.
embedding small extensions directly in the test), clean up the code, or
(hopefully) fix flakiness -- but they test the same behavior / use cases
as before.

BrowserActionApiTestWithContextType.UpdateSvg
  -> ExtensionActionAPITest.SetIconWithSvg
BrowserActionApiCanvasTest.InvisibleIconBrowserAction
  -> MultiActionAPICanvasTest.InvisibleIcon
BrowserActionApiTestWithContextType.IncognitoSplit
  -> ExtensionActionAPITest.IncognitoSplit
BrowserActionInteractiveTest.MAYBE_TestOpenPopupIncognito
  -> ActionAPIInteractiveUITest.OpenPopupInIncognitoWindow
     (hopefully no longer flaky!)
BrowserActionInteractiveTest.TestOpenPopupIncognitoFromBackground
  -> ActionAPIInteractiveUITest.OpenPopupInIncognitoWindow
     (rolled in with the test above)
BrowserActionInteractiveTest.TestOpenPopupDoesNotGrantTabPermissions
  -> ActionAPIInteractiveUITest.OpenPopupDoesNotGrantTabPermissions
BrowserActionInteractiveTest.OpenPopupOnPopup
  -> ActionAPIInteractiveUITest.OpenPopupFailsInWindowWithoutToolbar

Bug: 345091943, 571879165, 40804030

Correct Classic WebDriver pointer dimension WPT expectations

WebDriver accepts nonnegative Numbers for pointer width and height.
Move 0.1 and 2**53 from the invalid-input matrices to acceptance
coverage, alongside zero and omitted dimensions. Preserve genuine
invalid inputs and balance pointer button state between accepted values.

Remove the obsolete dimension-failure baselines and the invalid-input
file's failure expectation. Keep the separate wheel-ordering expectation.

[WebUI] Add isFaviconUrl() and use it in the searchbox

Searchbox components detect favicon images with
startsWith('chrome://favicon2/'). In extension pages getFaviconUrl()
returns a chrome-extension://<id>/_favicon/ URL, so those checks fail.

Add isFaviconUrl() to //resources/js/icon.ts, next to
getBaseFaviconUrl(), which recognizes both forms, and use it in
searchbox_icon.ts and searchbox_compose_button.ts.

No behavior change in WebUI.

a6b4aac2c201f03ca885b4caf26f2e6de06fc56b
parent
10a9c323bcedd3a351398fcb10dc480514a5de51 [ diff ] 
WaaP: Fix and re-enable DragToDetachThenCancel with InitialWebUI

Re-enables DragToDetachThenCancel in tab_drag_interactive_uitest.cc
now that InitialWebUI is enabled by default on trunk, along with test
fixes for the two platform-specific failures uncovered when running the
test:

1. On macOS, ui_controls::SendKeyEventsNotifyWhenDone() previously
   dispatched key events synchronously via -[NSApplication sendEvent:].
   When called from a Chrome task while a drag/move loop is spinning
   inside -[NSApp nextEventMatchingMask:...] (CFRunLoopRunSpecific), a
   key event that cancels the drag quits the nested RunLoop inside
   DoWorkImpl() before CFRunLoopRunSpecific exits, which can leave
   MessagePumpCFRunLoopBase's WorkDeduplicator wedged when the outer
   loop resumes. Post synthesized key events via
   -[NSApplication postEvent:atStart:NO] when a mouse button is down or
   a drag session is active so the drag loop dequeues and dispatches
   them outside DoWorkImpl(). Also replace the raw nested RunLoop in
   WaitForDetachedWindowVisible() with PollState.
2. On Wayland compositors without window-drag protocol support (such as
   Weston on linux-wayland-weston-rel), IsMoveLoopSupported() is false
   and detached window dragging falls back to system DnD, which cannot
   cancel on Escape. Skip VerticalTabDragTest.DragToDetachThenCancel
   when !IsMoveLoopSupported(), matching existing fallback tab-drag
   checks in tab_drag_controller_interactive_uitest.cc.

tree
00f418e4da337899290583ca07dc05d84a72ca3b
parent
5f01ecf0f9abca84ef7702c9adf190b807dd58dc [ diff ] 
[Extensions] Allow component extensions to opt in to chrome://image

Component extensions that reuse WebUI components, such as the searchbox,
need chrome://image (SanitizedImageSource) to display remote images.
Today component extensions may only load chrome://resources,
chrome://theme and chrome://webui-test.

chrome://image can make credentialed requests (e.g. for Google Photos),
so rather than granting it to all component extensions, it is only
allowed for those that explicitly declare chrome://image/* in their
manifest's host_permissions. Manifest parsing already restricts
chrome:// host permissions to component extensions.

parent
6a3d4aa0a1d00642db07d77c434e9fb43145bfd2 [ diff ] 
[WebUI NTP on AL] Make the ComposeboxContextMenu test suite hermetic

Browser tests don't guarantee window focus. An OS-generated window blur
or resize closes the context menu mid-test, and one test waited on real
sleeps, so ComposeboxContextMenu was flaky and disabled on Android. The
same failures show up as flakes on the desktop bots, most often in
"keeps menu open during slow tab add".

- Drop trusted window blur/resize events in the "Context menu" suite,
  and add a test that a window blur still closes the menu.
- Drive the menu timers in "keeps menu open during slow tab add" with
  MockTimer instead of real sleeps. microtasksFinished() is a
  setTimeout(0) and would hang once MockTimer is installed; the
  updateComplete awaits that follow already flush microtasks.
- Assert the menu position right after showAt(), which positions the
  menu synchronously. Yielding first lets a stray window event close
  the menu.
- Re-enable ComposeboxContextMenu on Android.

68584bdbe16bc9c42687b581d1e971fe6ceebca3 [ diff ] 
[CodeHealth] Migrate WebCodecs AudioData to AudioBuffer spans

This CL replaces uses of deprecated `media::AudioBuffer::channel_data()`
in `blink::AudioData` and `AudioDataTest` with `interleaved_data()`,
`planar_channel()`, `interleaved_data_cast()`, and
`planar_channel_cast()`.

In `AudioData::copyTo()`, this replaces raw pointer `memcpy` and manual
size bounds checks with `copy_from_nonoverlapping()` and `subspan()`.
In `CopyToInterleaved()` and `CopyToPlanar()`, source channels and
samples are read via typed spans, removing an unused `raw_ptr` include.
Aligned destinations are written via `base::subtle::reinterpret_span()`,
which removes the remaining `UNSAFE_TODO`s. Unaligned destinations still
go through a temporary buffer (see crrev.com/c/8532312).

TAG=agy

Remove wexit_time_destructors config from //device

Since is_wexit_time_destructors_default is enabled by default for
Chromium builds, -Wexit-time-destructors is already part of
//build/config/compiler:default_warnings. This makes
//build/config/compiler:wexit_time_destructors a no-op. Remove all
redundant references to wexit_time_destructors in //device BUILD.gn
files.

Reland "[WebUI NTP on AL] Enable new_tab_page_browsertest on Android"

This is a reland of commit 956f5117c20b1b420b7ec40be18efcc6ffcd2c30

The original change was reverted in https://crrev.com/c/8548578 (commit
61acfb6d05701a1ea06e615b3db1d319b9e91590) because RealboxSearchbox and
RealboxNext failed on the Android Desktop builders that run desktop
images (android-desktop-17-x64-rel-emu-tests,
android-internal-desktop-x64-rel). The hardware builders (brya, hylia)
also failed ReducedMotion. The CQ builder android-desktop-x64-rel runs a
tablet image, so none of this showed up there.

Changes since the original, both Android-only in the NTP test fixtures:
- With a hardware keyboard attached, opening the NTP focuses the
  omnibox, so the page gets no focus events and tests that wait for
  them hang or fail. The fixtures now move focus back to the page
  before the test runs.
- Lab devices run with animations off, so pages prefer reduced motion
  and the ReducedMotion "not preferred" tests fail. The fixtures now
  pass --force-prefers-no-reduced-motion.

Original change's description:
> [WebUI NTP on AL] Enable new_tab_page_browsertest on Android
>
> Enable new_tab_page_browsertest.cc and composebox_test_files on Android
> Desktop when enable_webui_ntp is true.
>
> - Move new_tab_page_browsertest.cc from !is_android to enable_webui_ntp
>   in chrome/test/data/webui/BUILD.gn, and add a direct dep on
>   composebox/variations. The test already includes
>   composebox_fieldtrial.h, and on Android nothing else provides that
>   header through public deps, so gn check fails without it.
> - Include composebox_test_files unconditionally and guard Smart Tab
>   Sharing references with <if expr="not is_android">, since its
>   searchbox.mojom methods are [EnableIfNot=is_android].
> - Add installMetricsReporterMock() in realbox_test.ts so suites with
>   reportMetrics set don't call the unmocked PageMetricsHost.
> - Enable kNtpCustomizeWebUiAndroid in NewTabPageBrowserTest on Android.
> - On Android, remove the 5s wall-clock deadline from
>   waitForAddFileCallCount. Pasted files are uploaded one at a time, and
>   each image upload makes a sync IPC to the browser UI thread
>   (URL.createObjectURL). On the Android emulator that thread can stall
>   for seconds while the soft keyboard animates, so
>   ComposeboxUploadPasteTest could miss the deadline. On Android the
>   helper now waits for the calls and asserts the exact count; the C++
>   test timeout still catches real hangs. Desktop keeps the deadline.
> - In the RealboxSearchbox tab picker test, assert right after
>   handleFuseboxAction() resolves. It opens the real context menu, and
>   yielding first lets an OS-generated window blur or resize close the
>   menu and reset shareTabsFlyoutOpen.
> - In the RealboxSearchbox entity image test, let the real image request
>   settle before mocking its load. Browser tests have no external
>   network access, so the request fails, and its error event could land
>   after the mocked load and bring back the placeholder color.
> - Disable Logo, VoiceSearchCoherence, InvariantChecks (b:565815572) and
>   ComposeboxContextMenu (b:565815899) on Android.
> - Since kNtpOneGoogleBar is disabled by default on Android, disable
>   OgbThemingRemoveScrim and OgbScrim and skip the OGB tests in Theming
>   there (b:571327206).
> - Remove the unused history_clusters include.
>
> Fixed: b:557038008
> Bug: b:565815572, b:565815899, b:571327206

[Actor] Remove tab concept from LoadAndExtractContentTool

This CL changes LoadAndExtractContentTool to access actor surfaces
instead of tab handles.

Switch ToolDelegate's AddTab/HasTab/RemoveTab, whose only caller was
this tool, to their ActorSurfaceHandle equivalents. The tool keeps
each tab's surface handle and adds and removes the tab with it, which
works whether or not the tab still exists. Tests now check the task
by surface handle, since a closed tab no longer maps to its surface.

TAG=agy
CONV=d44aa6f8-d6e7-4119-8de6-dcbb837b2709

[Notifications] Reject empty notification ID

Previously, BlinkNotificationServiceImpl::ClosePersistentNotification
forwarded an empty notification_id directly to
PlatformNotificationContextImpl::DeleteNotificationData, triggering a
CHECK(!notification_id.empty()) crash in
NotificationDatabase::DeleteNotificationData when an untrusted or fuzzed
renderer sent an empty string over Mojo.

Validate that notification_id is non-empty in
ClosePersistentNotification and report a bad Mojo message when it is
empty, matching the existing token validation in
DisplayNonPersistentNotification and CloseNonPersistentNotification.

Fixed: 572157205
Test: BlinkNotificationServiceImplTest.ClosePersistentNotificationRejectsEmptyId

[User Ed] Add FET features for MVP interruptive and regulatory promos

Registers six new Feature Engagement Tracker features for Clank MVP
interruptive promos and regulatory dialogs:
- IPH_AndroidTipsOptInPromo
- IPH_AppLanguagePromo
- IPH_ArchivedTabsAutoDeletePromo
- IPH_FullscreenSigninPromo
- IPH_NotificationPermissionPromo
- IPH_SearchEngineChoicePromo

Configures the interruptive promos with a 7-day trigger cooldown and a
limit of at most 3 impressions over 360 days, and configures the
regulatory dialogs to always trigger when requested.

e8304768c8e718b500d139ea8ae9163b4c7ad44a [ diff ] 
[macOS notifications] Prevent GetDisplayedNotifications hangs on disconnect

Wrap GetDisplayedNotifications callbacks in NotificationDispatcherMojo
with mojo::WrapCallbackWithDefaultInvokeIfNotRun. If the notification
service disconnects while a request is in flight, the caller's callback
is invoked with an empty set instead of being silently dropped, which
prevents hanging NotificationPlatformBridgeMac's BarrierClosure (and
subsequently hanging ServiceWorkerRegistration.getNotifications()).

TAG=agy
CONV=babda820-3f7f-42f5-a4eb-e7cc590dc5e7

bb183cc0523c42f6139d4d72bc9fe03d5bdf6ec9

36ea95b1b5e7132b2e8851ee35d9e308bdd7ca61 [ diff ] 
NullAway: Enable WarnOnGenericInferenceFailure

Makes NullAway warn when it cannot infer a type argument because a
@Nullable value flows into a position inferred as non-null (e.g. "new
Pair<>(nonNull, nullable)" assigned to a Pair<A, B>). Previously such
calls were silently unchecked.

Fixes fall into a few buckets:
 * Declare the nullable type argument honestly, e.g.
   Pair<Boolean, @Nullable String>, Map<Integer, @Nullable Foo>,
   FutureTask<@Nullable Void>, WeakReference<@Nullable Foo>,
   List<? extends @Nullable Foo>, and propagate to callers.
 * assertNonNull() on arguments that are non-null in context
 * Objects.requireNonNull() -> assertNonNull() (requireNonNull is not
   understood by the inference).

Behavior note: ChromeOriginVerifier now passes a null fingerprint list
rather than a one-element [null] list when a package has no signature
(only happens for non-existent packages in tests, which are handled by
the verification override path).

Skipped presubmit since the webapk shell_apk change is annotation-only
(no bytecode change), so its version does not need to be revved.

[omnibox][composebox] Move `aim_app` config into `cr-omnibox-composebox`

`omnibox-aim-app` currently configures its `cr-omnibox-composebox`
(flags -> properties, the Tall/Compact layout fallback, voice prompt
sizing, submit handling) and also wires it to `OmniboxPopupAimHandler`.
Move the configuration into `cr-omnibox-composebox` so that
`omnibox-full-app` can host the same element for the merged Full + AIM
popup without the composebox knowing which handler it talks to.

Key changes:
- `cr-omnibox-composebox` reads its AIM config from `loadTimeData`,
  derives its layout mode from `hasAllowedInputs`, handles its own
  submit / voice prompt events (the prompt state is a reflected
  `has-permission-prompt_` attribute, which the `aim_app.css` selector
  now keys on), and exposes `setContextMenuOpen()`.
- `omnibox-aim-app` is reduced to a wiring shell: it binds
  `omnibox_popup_aim.mojom`, forwards `close-composebox` /
  `context-menu-entrypoint-click` to the handler, and drives the
  composebox through its public API. `closeOnEscape` stays bound by the
  host since it depends on which popup hosts the composebox. The
  reflected properties that `aim_app.css` keys on stay until the
  stylesheet moves.
- Test hooks move with the logic: `webui_omnibox_interactive_uitest.cc`
  sets `hasAllowedInputs` / `setBaseSearchboxLayoutModeForTesting()` on
  `#composebox`; `omnibox_composebox_test.ts` pins the `loadTimeData`
  values the constructor reads and accounts for the submit cleanup the
  shell used to do.

No intended behavior change. The DOM path
`omnibox-aim-app > #composebox` is unchanged.

Demo:
http://screencast/cast/NTQyNDgzMTUwNjkzOTkwNHxjYjYwZTI5MS0xMQ

[User Ed] Add FeatureProperties API and cooldown/policy evaluation

Adds UiFormat and FeatureProperties to configure UI format, enterprise
policy compliance, and cooldown group participation on Tracker.

Updates Tracker#shouldTriggerHelpUi and
Tracker#shouldTriggerHelpUiWithSnooze to accept FeatureProperties,
marking the single-argument overloads @Deprecated.

In TrackerImpl:
- Evaluates native feature rules first to preserve failure metrics.
- Enforces the PromotionsEnabled enterprise policy when
  isBlockedByEnterprisePolicy is true, recording
  FAILURE_BLOCKED_BY_ENTERPRISE_POLICY.
- Evaluates format cooldown groups (IPH_BlueBubbleCooldownGroup and
  IPH_InterruptiveIphCooldownGroup), recording CooldownGroupActive,
  BlockedByCooldownGroup, and BypassedCooldownGroup user actions.
- Triggers and immediately dismisses the cooldown group feature when
  blocksCooldownGroup is true (or when AggressivelyCooldownIphs is
  enabled on deprecated overloads).

DD: go/clank-user-education-framework-design-doc

ce3673b6f3ab98666978fa4193f09f27edc0997f

6635e839f9033f3a562fdf09039fe61f397318e0 [ diff ] 
Create always-on-top window when requested by renderer

This CL changes browser_navigator to check for the newly-added
`always_on_top` WindowFeature when creating a popup window. If it
is set to `true` and the opener window has the `window_management`
permission, then it will create a Browser of type ALWAYS_ON_TOP_POPUP.
This new Browser type reuses the same frame as a document
picture-in-picture browser window, but is not connected to the
PictureInPictureWindowManager and can outlive the opener window.

This is part of a prototype for the "Creating always-on-top windows"
feature, which is behind a disabled-by-default Blink feature.

Chromestatus: https://chromestatus.com/feature/5200904761507840
Explainer: https://github.com/w3c/window-management/blob/main/EXPLAINER_always_on_top.md
Intent to Prototype: https://groups.google.com/a/chromium.org/g/blink-dev/c/1hrQn7ACjhI

57499ad105c1ec4dccbfd46e78a827e70cafbfb0
parent
906dc2ae8126db47a7d60fffe4653c416ae3c0d2 [ diff ] 
[OpenScreen] Remove unneeded pylint pragmas from media perf scripts

Of the 49 pylint pragma lines in chrome/test/media/performance, most had
no effect:

- broad-exception-caught (19), import-error and wrong-import-position
  (11), and too-many-locals (2) are already disabled by the depot_tools
  pylintrc.
- consider-using-with on `return subprocess.Popen(...)` never fires.
- Several standalone "# pylint: disable=" lines applied to the rest of
  their scope (for too-many-locals, the rest of the file). One sat on a
  subprocess.run it could not apply to.

This removes those and fixes the rest in code where possible:

- common.py declares its re-exported API in __all__ instead of
  disabling unused-import.
- remote_transport gains reset_connectivity_cache() so the test fakes
  no longer reach into its private cache.
- cros_setup.install_chromedriver is public, and the tests exercise
  cleanup and local installs through public methods.
- Access to Crossbench's private Selenium driver goes through a single
  helper instead of a module-wide protected-access disable.
- Imports that depend on common/perf_config setting up sys.path say so
  in a comment.

Seven suppressions remain (9 pragma lines), each on the specific
statement: Crossbench private members (2), Crossbench imports that must
follow sys.path setup (2), and three Popen calls whose processes
outlive the function.

No behavior change. Narrowing the broad except clauses themselves is
left to a follow-up.

Restore installer strings dep to interactive_ui_tests on Windows
📋 Metadata
commit
88dbf83d46433f0b2831f2760eaf049fc66bb9f9
[ log ]
author
David Bienvenu <davidbienvenu@chromium.org>
Wed Oct 14 00:20:02 2026
committer
Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Wed Oct 14 00:20:02 2026
tree
5d04db2e545c59e79e86e93df10dcf55cd00472c
parent
a5f4b7af4146fb28df0f986723c50d9d5fcfefda [ diff ] 
Restore installer strings dep to interactive_ui_tests on Windows

https://crrev.com/c/8520219 trimmed leaf deps from interactive_ui_tests
in chrome/test/BUILD.gn, including //chrome/installer/util:strings.

When interactive_ui_tests runs elevated as Administrator on Windows bots
(such as the win64 builder), ChromeTestLauncherDelegate::PreRunTests()
constructs ChromeTestLauncherDelegate::ScopedFirewallRules, which calls
installer::FirewallManager::Create() -> installer::GetLocalizedString().
Because PreRunTests() runs in the test launcher process before any
TranslationDelegate is installed, GetLocalizedString() loads the
localized string from the binary's Win32 RT_STRING resources and hits
NOTREACHED() when //chrome/installer/util:strings is not linked into
interactive_ui_tests.exe.

See https://ci.chromium.org/ui/p/chrome/builders/ci/win64/138972/overview for a sample failing test run.

5a5ef6e030a33fc39749fc51f91bcdfb709d7a14 [ diff ] 
Enable AudioCaptureAllowed enterprise policy on Android

Enable the AudioCaptureAllowed policy on Android (157-) and move the
key::kAudioCaptureAllowed -> prefs::kAudioCaptureAllowed mapping into
the all-platforms section of the configuration policy handler list.
The underlying preference registration and enforcement in
MediaStreamDevicePermissionContext and VoiceRecognitionUtil are
already present on Android.

Also register a PrefChangeRegistrar observer for
Pref.AUDIO_CAPTURE_ALLOWED in VoiceRecognitionHandler so that Omnibox
and NTP voice search availability update dynamically when the policy
value changes at runtime.

[PWA] Fix shortcut creation on Mac

This CL restores the Finder reveal for create shortcut. It was
previously gated behind the new install dialog. But, this breaks when
trying to create a shortcut after/outside of install in chrome://apps.

This CL uses `SHORTCUT_CREATION_AUTOMATED` instead in
finalize_install_or_update_job, and keeps `SHORTCUT_CREATION_BY_USER`
for an intentional shortcut creation in chrome://apps, etc.

Move LaunchEcheApp and RelaunchLast from EcheAppManagerFactory to EcheAppManager

Move LaunchedAppInfo, LaunchEcheApp, RelaunchLast, LaunchWebApp,
SetLastLaunchedAppInfo, and GetLastLaunchedAppInfo from
EcheAppManagerFactory to EcheAppManager, and bind EcheAppManager's
member methods directly instead of passing callbacks to its constructor.

EcheAppManagerTest and remove eche_app_manager_factory_unittest.cc.

581fb4b190e2303dc30bf0b317b283ca9d9910b5

118c1290f1a4f680540b5fa181bd8ce31d5dad65 [ diff ] 
[WebAudio] Fix ConvolverNode channel synchronization and Reverb::Process

When assigning an impulse response buffer concurrently with channelCount
or channelCountMode updates, ConvolverHandler could leave a stale
desired output channel count on Output(0), passing mismatched channel
counts into Reverb::Process.

Changes:
- Unconditionally update Output(0).SetNumberOfChannels() inside
  ConvolverHandler::CheckNumberOfChannelsForInput().
- Reset Output(0) channel count in ConvolverHandler::SetBuffer() when
  the buffer is cleared or detached.
- Require num_output_channels == 1 for the 1 -> 1 -> 1 case and replace
  DUMP_WILL_BE_NOTREACHED() with DCHECK(false) in Reverb::Process().
- Add unit tests in ConvolverHandlerTest and ReverbTest.

Bug: 468919026
Fixed: 468919026
Test: blink_platform_unittests --gtest_filter=*Reverb*
Test: blink_unittests --gtest_filter=*Convolver*

[Omnibox] Implement NTP search box hint animation in SearchBoxMediator

Implements the educational hint animation sequence in SearchBoxMediator
by driving the PropertyModel properties (PLUS_BUTTON_ROTATION,
HINT_TEXT_ALPHA, and PLUS_BUTTON_TINT) via PropertyModelAnimatorFactory
and ValueAnimator.

The sequence spins the plus button 360° with an accent tint pulse and
crossfades the hint text through example prompts before restoring the
default search hint and resting view properties upon completion or
early teardown.

Doc: https://docs.google.com/document/d/14SaIgd9pwAXHaDubyeIOJjG11i9c0KE7gv5urE9E2bU/edit?tab=t.balxzpt7psa7

TAG=agy
CONV=83655f94-cee9-40a4-a6b0-67caefd5c073

[ContextualTasks] Polish Lens region chip styling

The Lens region chip previously rendered a 1px translucent border inside
its extension iframe, which visually clashed with the outer attachment
tray styling in the AIM input plate. Removing the default light and dark
mode borders keeps the crop thumbnail flush with the container while
retaining a `ButtonText` border and `forced-color-adjust: none` on the
close button in high-contrast mode.

Propagate right-to-left layout direction when `dir="rtl"` is set on the
host element or document context, and add Skia Gold pixel coverage for
the chip across light, dark, LTR, and RTL configurations.

Validate-Test-Flakiness: skip
